## Experiments (candidates, selected, rejected)

- **X-001** [selected] tests H-001 with v1_support_sort vs v0 (control), seeds 0-7 matched per scene on 24 scenes, 384 episodes. Expected learning: Full-tier test of H-001: 24 dev scenes x 8 seeds x 2 methods. Per tier n=48 per method -> 95% Wilson CI about +/-0.09 near p=0.9, enough to check the >=0.8 per-tier falsifier and roughly the 0.05-of-v0 gap. Also logs planned order vs support graph on all 24 scenes (deterministic check of the second falsifier). Pass -> rung 1 is done and the next cycle can go to rung 5. Fail on T2 bridge/T3 pyramid -> fix the settle test/support_sort first.. Cost: 384 episodes (25.6% of 1500). Wall time about 0.4-2.4 min of compute on 8 workers (0.5-3 s/ep); Sumo at ~2.5x slower than real time could make that about 5-20 min.. X-001 is the only feasible option that can resolve H-001's per-tier >=0.8 and within-0.05-of-v0 criteria: n=48 per tier per method gives a CI of about +/-0.09, versus +/-0.15 for X-002. It costs 25.6% of the budget, uses validated code, and decides whether rung 1 closes. X-002 is cheaper but could not resolve these criteria and would likely need a top-up to the same n. X-003 is blocked: there is no v5 yaml or prompt yet, so validate_method fails. Write the rung-5 prompt in parallel and run it next cycle.
- **X-002** [rejected] tests H-001 with v1_support_sort vs v0 (control), seeds 0-2 matched per scene on 24 scenes, 144 episodes. Expected learning: Cheap screen of H-001: 24 dev scenes x 3 seeds x 2 methods. It still checks order validity on every scene. Per tier n=18 -> CI about +/-0.15, so it can catch a gross failure (a tier < 0.6) but cannot resolve the >=0.8 / within-0.05 criteria. A clean pass would probably need a top-up to reach X-001's n anyway.. Cost: 144 episodes (9.6%). Wall time about 5-8 min including Sumo slowdown.. 
- **X-003** [rejected] tests H-002 with rung-5 perceive=vlm (eval_prompt perception-only on 24 dev images, oracle order + scripted_weld) vs v0 control, seeds 0-2 on 24 scenes, 144 episodes. Expected learning: Perception-only pilot: 24 VLM image->JSON calls (0 sim episodes) scored against the oracle spec for block count/type and per-block pose error (3 cm / 10 deg), then a sim run of v5 vs v0 on 24 scenes x 3 seeds x 2 methods. It would test H-002's tier pattern directly: T0 >= 0.8 and T2/T3 low. It also bears on E-006/E-007, which suggest using a VLM for labels plus geometric pose estimation.. Cost: 144 sim episodes (9.6%) plus 24 VLM calls (API cost and latency). Writing the prompt and the method yaml first adds about one cycle of designer time.. 
- **X-004** [selected] tests H-002, H-003 with v5_vlm seeds 0-7 on 24 dev scenes (192). Control: v0 reused from R-003/R-005/R-007 (clean cbd9b16, seeds 0-7). v0 T3 is re-run at the clean HEAD, seeds 0-7 (48) to replace dirty R-009. Plus a 3-episode v0 determinism check at HEAD (T0-dev-01, T1-dev-01, T2-dev-01, seed 0) that must be bit-identical to R-003/5/7 before reuse is accepted. on 24 scenes, 243 episodes. Expected learning: This is a full-tier test of H-002 with n=48 per tier per method (CI about +/-0.09 near p=0.9). Perception is already 24/24 for count and within 3 cm, so H-002's pessimistic prediction (T2 <= 0.5, T3 <= 0.3) is likely falsified in the optimistic direction. The open question is whether the VLM lateral offset (up to 2.8 cm on T1-dev-01) stacks with v0's roughly 2 cm placement error and pushes T1 blocks past 3 cm. If T1 is below v0 by more than 0.05, the next step is a hybrid or offset-refinement rung (H-003's sim arm). If all tiers are >= 0.8 and within 0.05 of v0, rung 5 closes with VLM-only and H-003's hybrid is unnecessary. Note: H-003's hybrid-vs-VLM comparison is NOT covered, because no hybrid yaml exists. This run only gives the VLM-only reference arm.. Cost: 243 sim episodes (16% of 1500; 22% of the 1115 left), leaving 872. VLM calls come from the warm cache (24/24), so there is no API cost. Wall time is about 2-12 min on 8 workers, with no Sumo involved.. X-004 has the same power as X-006 (n=48 per tier, CI about +/-0.09), enough to resolve the >= 0.8 and within-0.05 criteria and the T1 offset risk, at 243 instead of 384 episodes. It reuses the clean v0 runs R-003/5/7, re-runs only the dirty T3 control (R-009), and adds a 3-episode determinism check. X-005 (72 episodes) cannot resolve the criteria, would need a top-up, and its T3 control is dirty. X-004 leaves 872 episodes for the rung-1 shuffled retests and the T0 runs for rungs 2/3/4. H-003's hybrid arm is not covered because no hybrid yaml exists.
- **X-005** [rejected] tests H-002, H-003 with Screen: v5_vlm seeds 0-2 on 24 dev scenes (72). Control: v0 seeds 0-2 subset reused from R-003/R-005/R-007. T3 control taken from dirty R-009 and flagged as such. on 24 scenes, 72 episodes. Expected learning: Per tier n=18 gives a CI of about +/-0.15. That catches a gross failure (a tier < 0.6, e.g. T1-dev-01 blowing the 3 cm limit) but cannot resolve the >= 0.8 or within-0.05 criteria. A pass would need a top-up to n=48 anyway, so it would likely cost 192 or more in total.. Cost: 72 episodes (about 5%). Wall time about 1-4 min.. 
- **X-006** [rejected] tests H-002, H-003 with v5_vlm vs v0, both fully re-run at the clean HEAD, seeds 0-7 on 24 dev scenes (192 + 192) on 24 scenes, 384 episodes. Expected learning: Same statistical power as X-004 (n=48 per tier per method), with a same-commit control and no reliance on determinism. Given RS-001's bit-identical result, it adds almost nothing over X-004's 3-episode determinism check.. Cost: 384 episodes (26%; 34% of what is left), leaving 731. That squeezes the rung-1 shuffled retests and the T0 runs for rungs 2/3/4 (Sumo is about 2.5x slower).. 
- **X-007** [selected] tests  with v3_grasp on 6 scenes, 6 episodes. Expected learning: Rung-3 implementation smoke: confirm physical handle contact grasp executes end to end and classify slip, drop, or handle collision.. Cost: Six charged T0-dev episodes, one seed per scene.. 
- **X-008** [selected] tests  with v3_grasp seeds 0-7 on T0-dev-01..06 + T1-dev-01..06 (96). Control: v0 reused from R-003 (T0) and R-005 (T1), clean cbd9b16, seeds 0-7, matched scenes and seeds. Reuse is justified by the determinism check in R-010. Report seed 0 on T0 separately as in-sample (calibration); seeds 1-7 (42 T0 episodes) are the out-of-sample estimate. on 12 scenes, 96 episodes. Expected learning: Two decisions. (1) T0 out of sample: n=42 on seeds 1-7, plus the in-sample seed 0. If T0 >= 0.9 and within 0.05 of v0 (1.00), the grasp generalises beyond its calibration pairs. If there is slip or drop on new seeds (like the 5.9 cm slip on T0-dev-02), the grasp calibration is overfit, so recalibrate before going further. (2) T1, n=48: is the post-release fall on T1-dev-01 specific to that scene/seed, specific to cube_on_brick (dev-01/03/05), or general across T1 (stacks dev-02/04/06 too)? Scene-specific: fix that release offset locally. Structure-specific: fix the release/retreat for small tops on long supports. General: rung 3 is blocked at T1, so fix the release/settle stage before any T2/T3 spend. T1-dev-01 alone failing on all 8 seeds caps T1 at 0.875, so the gap to v0 (1.00) would already exceed 0.05. The run is diagnostic, not a pass/fail gate. Per-tier Wilson CI is about +/-0.09.. Cost: 96 episodes (6.4% of 1500; 10.5% of the 914 left), leaving 818. Real-grasp contact physics is slower than weld. R-011 ran 6 episodes in 10 s, so wall time is about 3-8 min on 8 workers. No API cost.. X-008 (96 episodes, T0+T1 seeds 0-7, with the reused matched v0 controls R-003/R-005) answers both open rung-3 questions in one cycle. Caveat (1): seeds 1-7 give an out-of-sample T0 estimate (n=42) away from the calibration pairs. Caveat (2): T1 n=48 shows whether the post-release fall is limited to T1-dev-01, to cube_on_brick, or general across T1, which decides where the release fix goes. X-009 (48) settles only caveat (1) and would need a T1 follow-up anyway, so it costs the same total over two cycles. X-010 (192) spends 96 extra episodes on T2/T3, which will almost certainly fail from the same known release defect, and v3 has not been validated on T2/T3. Budget: 914 left now (X-004 has finished: R-010 to R-016, 243 episodes), so 818 remain after this run.
- **X-009** [rejected] tests  with v3_grasp seeds 0-7 on T0-dev-01..06 (48). Control: v0 R-003 reused (seeds 0-7, matched). Seed 0 is flagged in-sample. on 6 scenes, 48 episodes. Expected learning: Gives an out-of-sample T0 estimate on seeds 1-7 (n=42), which answers caveat (1) only. It says nothing about the known T1-dev-01 post-release failure, which is the more likely blocker for rung 3. A T0 pass still leaves the T1 question open, so a second T1 run (about 48) would be needed anyway, for the same total of about 96 over two cycles.. Cost: 48 episodes (5.3% of the 914 left). About 2-4 min.. 
- **X-010** [rejected] tests  with v3_grasp seeds 0-7 on 24 dev scenes T0-T3 (192). Control: v0 reused from R-003/R-005/R-007 and the clean T3 rerun R-012, all matched. on 24 scenes, 192 episodes. Expected learning: This is the full rung-3 gate. But with a known T1 release failure, T2/T3 (bridges, towers, pyramids, all with stacked tops) will very likely fail for the same release cause, so the extra 96 episodes mostly re-measure that known defect. They would be informative only after the release fix.. Cost: 192 episodes (21% of the 914 left), leaving 722. T2/T3 contact physics is slow, about 10-25 min.. 
- **X-011** [selected] tests H-001 with v1_support_sort vs v0 (control), seeds 0-7, matched scenes+seeds, paired on 16 scenes, 256 episodes. Expected learning: The real H-001 test: the listed and lexical orders both violate support, so support_sort has to do real work. Order falsifier: any v1 planned order that violates support -> H-001 falsified and rung 1 reopens; order is deterministic, so a single seed decides it. Success falsifier: per-tier n = T1 48, T2 32, T3 48 per arm. Wilson LB >= 0.8 holds with 1 failure on T2 (31/32 -> LB 0.84) and with 2 on T1/T3. Base failure rate is ~4% from placement_error, so this is the smallest n where one expected placement miss does not make the T2 result inconclusive. A paired v1-v0 discordance count shows whether a different valid order changes placement outcomes. Pass -> rung 1 fully closed (upgrades D-001 from provisional). Fail -> fix the planner before any rung that drops oracle_build_order.. Cost: 256 episodes (128/arm), ~30% of the 866 left. Wall time ~3-15 min on 8 workers (scripted weld, no Sumo). Leaves ~610, well above the 48 reserved for rung 3 T0.. Both candidates settle the deterministic order falsifier equally well. Only the 8-seed run also settles the per-tier >=0.8 success claim: T2 n=32 tolerates one placement_error (LB 0.84), but at n=16 a single miss (P~0.48) drops the LB to ~0.72 and forces a top-up, so the 4-seed run would likely cost about the same and give a messier record. 256 episodes is cheap (no Sumo, minutes of wall time) and leaves ~610, well above the 48 reserved for rung 3 T0. No negative control: no plan_order stage builds in the listed order (only llm/oracle/support_sort are registered), adding one would mean new code plus validation, and the deterministic no-sim check already shows the listed and lexical orders violate support on all 16 scenes.
- **X-012** [rejected] tests H-001 with v1_support_sort vs v0 (control), seeds 0-3, matched scenes+seeds, paired on 16 scenes, 128 episodes. Expected learning: The order falsifier is fully decided here too: order is deterministic and 4 seeds per scene is more than enough. The success falsifier is weak. Per-tier n = T1 24, T2 16, T3 24 per arm. On T2, 16/16 gives a Wilson LB of only 0.806, and one failure (15/16) gives an LB of ~0.72 < 0.8. At the ~4% placement_error base rate, P(>=1 T2 failure) is ~0.48, so about half the time the T2 tier comes out inconclusive and needs a top-up run. The paired discordance estimate is coarser.. Cost: 128 episodes (64/arm), ~15% of the 866 left. Wall time ~2-7 min on 8 workers. Leaves ~738.. 
- **X-013** [rejected] tests  with v4_place vs v0 (fresh control, same commit), seeds 0-3, matched scenes and seeds, paired by (scene, seed). Tests D-002/RS-002 (no rung-4 hypothesis has been filed yet). on 12 scenes, 96 episodes. Expected learning: Cheap T1-only probe of the mechanism. Primary metric is the per-block signed lateral error of the top block vs pick-side y (RS-002 b_y about +1.5/-1.9/-1.6 cm on dev-01/02/05). Outcomes are near-deterministic per scene, so 4 seeds x 12 scenes is enough for this. If v4_place |b_y| < 0.5 cm and median pos err < v0's 2.13 cm: the bias is removed, so next run v4_place+VLM on T1 and expect dev-02/05 to flip. If the bias persists: the closed loop is not correcting lateral error, so fix place_correction before any rung-5 combination. Success rate cannot discriminate here because v0 T1 is already 48/48. Limit: it says nothing about the T2/T3 placement_error episodes that v0 actually fails.. Cost: 96 episodes (48 per arm), 19% of 514. Wall time about 1-2 min (weld, no Sumo). Leaves 418, 268 above the 150 reserve.. 
- **X-014** [selected] tests  with v4_place, seeds 0-7, on all 34 T1/T2/T3 dev scenes (272). Control is v0 at matched scenes and seeds 0-7, reused from R-005 (T1-01..06), R-007 (T2-01..06), R-012 (T3-01..06) and R-017 (T1-07..12, T2-07..10, T3-07..12). Plus a fresh v0 drift gate at seed 0 on all 34 scenes (34). Per-scene pos error must match the reused controls within 0.01 mm, the same criterion as the R-010 gate. Tests D-002/RS-002. on 34 scenes, 306 episodes. Expected learning: This answers all three D-002 questions in one run. (1) T1 mechanism: per-block signed lateral error vs pick side, same as X-013 but with 8 seeds. (2) Whether the 7 known v0 placement_error episodes flip (T2 2/48 + 2/32, T3 2/48 + 1/48). All 7 sit at seeds 0-7 of the reused controls, so a paired (scene, seed) McNemar-style count on exactly those episodes is possible. A 4-seed design could miss them entirely. (3) Whether there is a regression anywhere (any v0 success that v4_place fails). Decisions: if the bias is gone and the 7 failures flip with no regressions, rung 4 (oracle-pose) is cleared on dev T1-T3 and the next step is v4_place+VLM on T1. If the bias is gone but T2/T3 failures persist, they have a second cause (bridge/tower settle), so ask the analyst for a separate failure analysis. If the bias persists, fix closed_loop_weld_place before any rung-5 combination. If the drift gate fails, rerun the control fresh: up to 238 more episodes, which would eat the reserve, so stop and re-plan.. Cost: 306 episodes (272 v4_place + 34 v0 gate), 60% of 514. Wall time about 5-10 min (weld; T2 ran about 46-131 s per 48). Leaves 208, 58 above the 150 reserve.. X-014 is the only candidate that tests every known v0 placement_error episode (7, at seeds 0-7 on T2/T3) with a paired (scene, seed) comparison. It also measures the T1 pick-side bias and checks for regressions, all in one run. The matched 8-seed v0 controls (R-005/R-007/R-012/R-017) already exist, so a 34-episode seed-0 drift gate replaces a 272-episode control rerun. X-013 (96) is cheap and settles the T1 mechanism, but v0 T1 is already 48/48, so success cannot discriminate there, and it leaves the T2/T3 question for another cycle. X-015 (272) costs almost the same as X-014 but covers only seeds 0-3, so it may miss most of the rare v0 failures and would need an unaffordable top-up. X-014 costs 306 and leaves 208, above the 150 reserve for the Sumo rungs 2/4 and the rung-3 fix. If the drift gate fails, stop and re-plan; do not rerun the full control. The result must state that v4_place adds oracle_block_pose (and drops scripted_arm), so any gain only holds with a pose oracle. Note: no rung-4 hypothesis exists, so method_designer should file one with falsifiers before the analyst reads the result.
- **X-015** [rejected] tests  with v4_place vs v0 (both fresh, same commit), seeds 0-3, on all 34 T1/T2/T3 dev scenes, matched and paired. Tests D-002/RS-002. on 34 scenes, 272 episodes. Expected learning: Same mechanism readout as X-014, with a clean same-commit control and no reliance on reuse. The weakness is coverage of the rare v0 failures: there are 7 placement_error episodes across seeds 0-7, and at most the seed 0-3 subset is covered (expected about 3-4, possibly 0-1). The "does v4_place fix the T2/T3 failures" question is therefore likely to come out underpowered or inconclusive, and a top-up to seeds 4-7 would be needed anyway (about 272 more, over budget).. Cost: 272 episodes (136 per arm), 53% of 514. About 5-10 min. Leaves 242.. 
- **X-016** [selected] tests H-004 with Physics A/B at seed 0 only (outcomes are nearly seed-invariant, so we spend on scenes, not seeds). Four arms:
(1) v0_elliptic, 40 scenes (T0-01..06 plus all 34 T1-T3 dev): 40 eps.
(2) v0 default, fresh, on the 28 scenes not covered by R-021: 28 eps. R-021 (v0, seed 0, current commit) is reused for T1-01..04, T2-01..04 and T3-01..04.
(3) v3_grasp default, fresh reproduction, on T0-01..06 and T1-01..12: 18 eps.
(4) v3_grasp_elliptic (re-tuned) on the same 18 scenes: 18 eps.
Physics config is the only difference between arms (1) and (2): same params, scenes and seed. Arms (3) and (4) differ in physics and also in the re-tune (as-is v3_grasp already slips 12/12 under elliptic, so it is not re-run).
Logged per block: success, final pos err (cm), ang err (deg), signed lateral err vs pick side, pre_release_err and post-settle err (cm), relative carry slip (cm), and failure category (slip/drop/placement_error/release).
Arms (1) and (2) can run as soon as v0_elliptic validates. Arms (3) and (4) are gated on validate_method passing for v3_grasp_elliptic.
Physics stays out of X-014. The fresh default v0 seed-0 runs (arm 2 plus R-021) cover all 34 T1-T3 scenes, so they can also serve as X-014's drift gate, which would cut X-014 from about 136 to about 114 more episodes. on 40 scenes, 104 episodes. Expected learning: This separates simulator creep from controller error, and does it before X-014 spends 136 episodes.
(a) v0 (arm 1 vs arm 2 + R-021, paired by scene; physics is the only factor): look at the change in per-block median pos err and the signed lateral bias. If elliptic cuts the median from about 2.1 cm by >= 0.5 cm, and/or removes the systematic lateral bias, then most of the 2 cm drift (RS-002/RS-003) is creep. In that case X-014 is re-planned under elliptic (v4_place_elliptic + v0_elliptic control) and every default-physics result gets a creep caveat. If the change is < 0.3 cm and success is paired-identical, the drift is scripted-arm error and X-014 resumes under default physics as-is.
(b) v3_grasp (arm 3 vs arm 4): if slip+drop on T0 falls from 7/48-rate to about 0 and T1 rises above 0, with remaining failures reclassified as placement/release, creep was a real share of the rung-3 failures, and H-004's release-mechanism test moves to elliptic. This contrast is confounded by the re-tune, so the clean physics attribution comes from (a) and the slip-category counts.
(c) Reproduction: the fresh default arms must match the old seed-0 episodes per scene within 0.01 mm. The references are R-003/R-005/R-007/R-012/R-017 for v0 and R-019/R-020 seed 0 for v3_grasp. If they don't match, the old controls are stale on current code and only fresh arms are used.. Cost: 104 episodes (68 v0 + 36 v3), within the ~120 cap, which leaves 398. Wall time on 8 workers: the v0 weld arms take about 1-20 min (R-017 ran at about 6400 eps/h, R-021 at about 180 eps/h on T3). The v3 arms take about 1 min (R-019/R-020 ran at about 4500-7500 eps/h). Elliptic with impratio 10 may be about 1.5-2x slower. No Sumo.. X-016 (104 eps, within the ~120 cap, leaves 398) answers both creep questions in one cycle.
(a) The v0 default vs elliptic pair changes physics only, at matched scenes and seed 0. It decides whether the 2 cm placement drift is a simulator artifact.
(b) The v3_grasp pair shows whether the slips and drops fall away under elliptic. It adds only 36 eps and about 1 min of wall time over X-017.
Both default arms are re-run fresh, so they double as reproduction checks of R-003/R-005/R-007/R-012/R-017 and R-019/R-020 at seed 0 on current code.
Rejected X-017 (68): it saves 36 eps but leaves the rung-3 slip question for another cycle.
Rejected X-018 (292): it is the only design that separates the v3 re-tune from physics, but it costs 2.4x the cap, needs a yaml that doesn't exist, and spends on seeds that add little.
The v3 contrast in X-016 is confounded by the re-tune, so attribute creep from the v0 arms and the slip/drop category counts.
Gating: the v0 arms run as soon as v0_elliptic validates. The v3 arms wait until v3_grasp_elliptic passes validate_method, and arms 3 and 4 run on the same commit.
X-014 recommendation: wait. Run the v0 arms first (minutes). Then:
- If v0_elliptic cuts the median pos err by >= 0.5 cm or removes the lateral bias, re-plan X-014 under elliptic (v4_place_elliptic vs v0_elliptic). v4_place would otherwise be tuned to cancel creep.
- If the change is < 0.3 cm with paired-identical success, resume X-014 under default physics as-is.
- Anything in between goes to the PI.
Waiting costs minutes. Resuming blind risks about 136 eps (27% of budget) on a simulator artifact.
The fresh default v0 seed-0 arm plus R-021 covers all 34 T1-T3 scenes, so it can serve as X-014's drift gate (saves about 22 eps).
method_designer should file a physics hypothesis with falsifiers before the analyst reads the result.

Expected learning: Gives the same clean physics-only answer on the 2 cm placement drift as X-016 arm (a), so it settles the X-014 resume/re-plan decision. It says nothing about the rung-3 slips and drops (R-019/R-020), so H-004 would need another cycle under elliptic.. Cost: 68 episodes, about 1-20 min wall time. Leaves 434..

The fourth cell needs a new v3_grasp_retuned_default.yaml. That is 4 x 18 x 2 = 144 eps. Total about 292. on 40 scenes, 292 episodes. - **X-019** [selected] tests  with Cheap T0-only probe. Run v2_walk (Sumo walking approach, then scripted weld) at seeds 0-3 on T0-dev-01..06, default physics: 24 episodes. The control is v0 reused from R-003, using the seed 0-3 rows for the same scenes; RS-004 shows these reproduce bit-exact on current code, so no fresh control is needed. Paired by (scene, seed). Per episode, log: success, approach error (base pose vs target: xy in cm, yaw in deg), final block pos/ang error, the per-episode block error difference v2 minus v0, signed lateral/longitudinal error components, and failure category. Render video for seed 0 only. on 6 scenes, 24 episodes. Expected learning: This isolates what walking adds to placement error, using single blocks with no stacking interactions. Decisions: (1) If 24/24 succeed (Wilson LB 0.86), the paired median block-error increase is <= 0.3 cm and max block error is <= 2.7 cm, then walking adds negligible error and rung 2 is cleared on T0. T1 then goes straight into the later rung-2+others combined run, with no separate T1 rung-2 run. (2) If block error grows by 0.3-0.8 cm, or approach error is > 1 cm on any seed, the 3 cm budget on stacked T1 tops (v0 already at about 2.1 cm) is at risk. In that case, run a targeted T1 probe (T1-dev-01..06, seed 0-1) before combining, or tighten the walk/approach correction first. (3) Any failure, or block error > 3 cm, means rung 2 is not cleared. Fix the approach/arm re-targeting from the actual base pose before spending more. Approach error vs block error correlation shows whether the arm compensates for base error (it should, since the arm re-plans from the true base pose) or inherits it.. Cost: 24 episodes (6% of the 398 left; about 284 left after X-014's ~114). Wall time: about 82 s per episode end to end (smoke). On 8 workers that is about 3 waves, roughly 5-15 min including Sumo at 2.5x real time; the serial worst case is about 33 min. Sumo slows other batches through CPU contention (RS-004 saw about 2.3 s wall per sim second, 5-7x), so schedule it after X-016's v0 arms finish and not alongside X-014. Results are deterministic, so contention costs only wall time, not validity.. X-019 (v2_walk on T0-dev-01..06 at seeds 0-3, 24 eps) isolates what walking adds to block error, with no stacking or bias confounds. The control is v0 R-003 at the matched seed 0-3 rows; RS-004 shows these reproduce bit-exact, so there is no control spend. It costs 6% of the 398 left, about 5-15 min on 8 workers (serial worst case about 33 min at about 82 s per episode), and leaves about 284 after X-014.

Decision rule:
- 24/24, a paired block-error increase <= 0.3 cm and max <= 2.7 cm: rung 2 is cleared on T0, and T1 joins the later combined run.
- An increase of 0.3-0.8 cm or an approach error > 1 cm: run a small T1 probe first, or fix re-targeting from the actual base pose.
- Any failure or > 3 cm: not cleared; fix before spending more.

Rejected X-020 (T0+T1, 48). T1 is the real 3 cm stress test, but a T1 result now would be confounded with the pick-side bias that X-014 is about to address and with the creep question in X-016/H-005, so it would likely be redone. T1 also has no smoke yet, and it doubles the Sumo time.

Rejected X-021 (T0 at seeds 0-7, 48). Its Wilson LB of 0.93 vs 0.86 does not change any decision; the paired error increment drives the decision, and 24 pairs already estimate it.

Scheduling: start after X-016's v0 arms and keep it apart from X-014. Sumo contention (RS-004, 5-7x wall time) costs only wall time, since results are deterministic. Render video on seed 0 only. Check that the sumo_to_assembly_state_bridge passes the true base pose and does not snap it. method_designer should file a rung-2 hypothesis with falsifiers before the analyst reads the result.

So a T1 result now may need to be redone after those land. T0 alone already gives the walking error increment that predicts T1 margin.. Cost: 48 episodes (12% of 398; about 236 left after X-014). Paired by (scene, seed). Logging is the same as the T0 probe. on 6 scenes, 48 episodes. Expected learning: It gives the standard n=48 tier gate: 48/48 has Wilson LB 0.93, against 0.86 at n=24. - **X-022** [selected] tests  with Rung-4 MPC T0 probe, run SERIALLY after X-019 finishes. Run v4_mpc (Sumo CEM placement; cost = block pose + upright + collision clearance + settle velocity + angular velocity + gripper contact height; success gate unchanged) at seeds 0-3 on T0-dev-01..06, default physics: 24 episodes. The control is v0 R-003 (cbd9b16), using the seed 0-3 rows, reused and paired by (scene, seed). T0 drift check at no episode cost: compare R-003's seed-0 rows with R-022's (fresh v0, T0-dev-01..06, seed 0, 911b67d+dirty). They must match within 0.01 mm. Note that RS-004 checked only T1-T3, not T0. Per episode, log: success, final block pos/ang error after the 5 s settle, the paired difference vs R-003, signed x/y error, Sumo terminal block pose, assembly-world block pose immediately after the bridge (before settle), the bridge transfer delta (assembly pre-settle minus Sumo terminal), post-bridge settle drift, CEM iterations completed, wall time, timeout flag, and failure category. Render video on seed 0. BRIDGE AUDIT (before the analyst reads results): the engineer confirms in code whether sumo_to_assembly_state_bridge writes the Sumo terminal block qpos (object_pose_qpos_index 26) into the lab block's free joint, i.e. teleports it. The engineer also confirms whether object_goal / object_goal_quat (fixed in the yaml at [0.15, 0, 0.254]) are overwritten per scene from the oracle spec. on 6 scenes, 24 episodes. Expected learning: This tests whether MPC placement in Sumo, as handed to the assembly world, matches or beats v0's ~2.35 cm T0 error. The smoke result was 0.87 cm. Each outcome below changes a decision. The outcome is reported as a property of v4_mpc *with the state bridge*, not as physical placement in the lab world.

PRE-REGISTERED RULE. Rules are checked in this order: VOID, then NOT CLEARED, then AT RISK, then CLEARED. "Improves" is checked only if the result is CLEARED.

- VOID: the bridge audit finds that the assembly block pose is set to the target or snapped (not copied from the Sumo terminal state), OR object_goal is not set per scene. In that case success is not attributable to the MPC, so stop and fix before any reading.
- NOT CLEARED: any failure, OR any block > 3 cm or > 10 deg.
- AT RISK: no failure, but max block error is in (2.7, 3.0] cm, OR the paired median error is worse than R-003 by > 0.3 cm, OR any |bridge transfer delta| > 0.5 cm, OR median post-bridge settle drift > 0.5 cm (the teleported state is not at rest), OR any episode hits the 600 s timeout.
- CLEARED: 24/24, max block error <= 2.7 cm, max ang error <= 10 deg, and paired median change vs R-003 <= +0.3 cm.
- IMPROVES on v0 (only if CLEARED): paired median reduction >= 0.5 cm, AND >= 19/24 pairs improved (sign test p < 0.01), AND >= 5/6 scenes improved at the scene median, because effective n = 6 scenes.

Decisions:
- CLEARED + IMPROVES: v4_mpc becomes a rung-4 contender. Its T1 run waits for X-014/H-006, then compares against v4_place on matched T1 scenes and seeds.
- CLEARED without improvement: park v4_mpc, since v4_place gives the same at far lower cost.
- AT RISK: no T1 spend; the engineer looks at the bridge/settle or cost terms.
- NOT CLEARED: fix before more spend.. Cost: 24 episodes (6% of the 398 left; ~260 left after X-019 (24) and X-014 (~114)). Smoke run: ~16 s end to end. On 8 workers that is 3 waves, about 1-2 min uncontended and about 5-10 min under contention (RS-004 measured 5-7x). Worst case is the 600 s timeout: 3 waves x 600 s = 30 min. Running serially after X-019 adds only those minutes.. X-022: v4_mpc, T0-dev-01..06, seeds 0-3, 24 eps, run SERIALLY after X-019. The control is R-003 at the matched seed 0-3 rows, paired. The T0 drift check costs nothing: compare R-022's fresh v0 seed-0 T0 rows with R-003. This matters because RS-004 covered only T1-T3, so it never showed that R-003 itself reproduces. Budget: 398 left, then about 236 after X-019 (24), X-014 (~114) and this run.

(1) BRIDGE. The block is almost certainly teleported: the bridge copies the Sumo terminal block qpos (index 26) into the lab world. sumo_to_assembly_state_bridge is already listed, but its text must say "lab block set to Sumo terminal pose". Two more items must be added to v4_mpc's idealizations: no_arm_block_collision (the yaml has arm_block_collision: false) and oracle_block_pose (the MPC cost reads true block qpos). The result must name all of them and report the bridge transfer delta and the post-bridge settle drift. If the audit finds the block snapped to its target, or object_goal not set per scene (it is fixed in the yaml), the result is VOID.

(2) Run serially, not alongside X-019. Contention leaving results unchanged was shown only for v0 MuJoCo. A CEM planner tied to wall-clock time could get fewer iterations under load. Running serially costs only about 5-10 min, so there is no reason to spend X-023's 6 determinism episodes and risk a 24-episode rerun.

(3) RULE, checked in order: VOID, then NOT CLEARED (any failure, > 3 cm or > 10 deg), then AT RISK (max in (2.7, 3.0] cm, paired median worse by > 0.3 cm, |bridge delta| > 0.5 cm, settle drift > 0.5 cm, or any timeout), then CLEARED (24/24, max <= 2.7 cm, paired median change <= +0.3 cm). IMPROVES requires a paired median reduction >= 0.5 cm, >= 19/24 pairs improved and >= 5/6 scenes.
- CLEARED + IMPROVES: T1 head-to-head vs v4_place after X-014.
- CLEARED only: park v4_mpc.
- Otherwise: fix before spending.

Rejected X-024 (12): 12 pairs and 2 seeds cannot reach the IMPROVES bar, so a top-up to 24 would be likely. method_designer should file this rule as a hypothesis before the first run.

- **X-025** [selected] tests H-010 with Full pre-registered design. v3_grasp_lift (release_vertical_lift_m 0.125, 12 steps, no oracle correction, default physics) at seeds 0-7 on T0-dev-01..06 and T1-dev-01..06: 96 episodes, both tiers on one commit. Control: v3_grasp R-019 (T0) / R-020 (T1), same scenes and seeds 0-7, paired by (scene, seed). v0 R-003 / R-005 for reference. No fresh control spend. Validity check for the reuse: X-016 arm 3 (fresh v3_grasp seed 0, T0+T1-01..06) must match R-019/R-020 seed 0 within 0.01 mm. If it does not match, or arm 3 has not run, add 12 eps of fresh v3_grasp seed 0 on these 12 scenes before reading. Per episode, log: success, failure category, pre_release_err, per-phase block displacement (release_open / vertical_lift / retreat) for every block, handle_contacts_after_close, transit drift. Render video on T1-dev-01 s1, T1-dev-02 s1 and T0-dev-05 s3, which were the RS-005 exemplars. Report: Wilson 95% per tier; seed 0 (in-sample calibration seed) separately from seeds 1-7; per-scene k/8 and scene-level counts (effective n = 6 per tier); the mechanism check (post-release displacement < 1 cm on >= 90% of episodes that reached release without a pre-release drop). on 12 scenes, 96 episodes. Expected learning: This is H-010 exactly as pre-registered, with no threshold remapping. T1 >= 39/48 and T0 >= 39/48 (T0-dev-02 drops still count as failures) mean SUPPORTED. In that case rung 3 release is fixed, T2/T3 spend can resume on v3_grasp_lift, and the next defect is the brick transit drop (elliptic+noslip, X-016). T1 < 39/48 with the mechanism check failing means the lift does not clear the jaw. The engineer then looks at lift height or approach geometry, and no T2/T3 spend happens. T1 < 39/48 with the mechanism check passing means the remaining failures are something else (dev-03 brick drops, the dev-02 handle miss), which goes to the grasp work. If T0 is refuted only by dev-02 drops above 7 while no other T0 failures remain, that is recorded as out-of-scope drop variance. 8 seeds per scene resolve the seed-dependent scenes (T0-dev-05 was 4/8, T0-dev-02 1/8, T1-dev-05 split base-shove vs top-knock by seed). The expected T0 result of about 41/48 sits only 2 above the bar, so a smaller n would probably come out inconclusive. Out-of-sample seeds 1-7 give 42 episodes per tier; the secondary out-of-sample bar is >= 34/42 (0.81).. Cost: 96 episodes (24% of 397). After the pending X-019 (24), X-022 (24) and X-014 (~114), 235 remain; this leaves ~139, or ~127 if the 12-episode repro top-up is needed. Wall time: R-019/R-020 took 23 s and 38 s for 48 episodes each. With the extra lift phase and a 5 s settle, about 1-3 min total on 8 workers. No Sumo, but run it outside the X-019/X-022 Sumo windows to avoid contention.. X-025: v3_grasp_lift, seeds 0-7, T0-dev-01..06 + T1-dev-01..06, 96 eps. This is H-010 as pre-registered, so there is no threshold remapping and no deviation. Control: R-019/R-020, paired by (scene, seed), reused at no episode cost; v0 R-003/R-005 for reference. Budget: 397 - X-019 (24) - X-022 (24) - X-014 (~114) = 235, so this leaves ~139, or ~127 with the 12-episode repro top-up. Wall time is ~1-3 min. Launch is gated on: (a) a smoke run (T1-dev-01 s1) showing a z rise of >= 0.12 m before any lateral motion, with the per-phase displacement logs populated; and (b) X-016 arm 3 reproducing R-019/R-020 at seed 0, or else 12 fresh v3_grasp seed-0 episodes. Run it outside the Sumo windows. Report seed 0 (in-sample) separately; the out-of-sample secondary bar is >= 34/42. Also report per-scene k/8 (effective n = 6 per tier) and the mechanism check.

Rejected X-026 (seeds 0-3, 48 eps). Keeping the 0.8 rate maps 39/48 to >= 20/24, a deviation from the pre-registration. T0 is predicted at ~41/48, just above a 39/48 bar, so 24 episodes would probably come out borderline and force a 48-episode top-up. It also doubles seed 0's in-sample weight and does not raise the scene-level n. It saves 48 episodes nominally, and probably none in practice.

Rejected X-027 (T1 first). It saves episodes only if T1 and the mechanism check both fail, which the RS-005 prior makes unlikely. T0's ground-drag test is informative either way, and the staging adds a cycle and a risk of commit drift for no wall-time gain.
- **X-026** [rejected] tests H-010 with Half-seed design. v3_grasp_lift at seeds 0-3 on T0-dev-01..06 and T1-dev-01..06: 48 episodes (24 per tier). Control: the R-019/R-020 seed 0-3 rows, paired. Logging, video, mechanism check and repro check are the same as X-025. THRESHOLD MAPPING (DEVIATION from pre-registration, which must be filed before the run): the bar stays the same rate, 39/48 = 0.8125, so >= 20/24 per tier (0.833; 19/24 = 0.79 would lower the bar). The mechanism bar is unchanged at >= 90% of eligible episodes. on 12 scenes, 48 episodes. Expected learning: It detects a gross T1 effect (0/24 -> ~20/24) just as well as the full design, since a 0 -> 0.8 shift needs few episodes. But it is weak exactly where H-010 is tight. (1) T0 is predicted at ~0.85 against a 0.81 bar, so with 24 episodes one extra dev-02 drop or dev-05 drag flips the verdict. A borderline result would force a top-up of seeds 4-7 (+48), so the total ends up at 96 over two cycles. (2) Seed 0 is in-sample, and it carries 1/4 of the weight here vs 1/8 in the full design. Out-of-sample is only 18 per tier (bar >= 15/18). (3) It samples only 4 seeds on the seed-dependent scenes (T0-dev-05, T0-dev-02, T1-dev-05). (4) The effective n stays 6 scenes per tier either way, so halving the seeds saves episodes without changing scene-level power, while per-scene rates get coarser (k/4).. Cost: 48 episodes (12% of 397), leaving ~187 after pending work. About 1 min of wall time. Expected real cost is higher, because a borderline T0 (likely given the 41/48 prediction) brings it to 96 over two cycles.. 
- **X-027** [rejected] tests H-010 with Staged, T1 first. Stage 1: v3_grasp_lift at seeds 0-7 on T1-dev-01..06 (48 eps) vs R-020. Stage 2, only if T1 >= 39/48 or the mechanism check passes: seeds 0-7 on T0-dev-01..06 (48 eps) vs R-019. If T1 fails AND the mechanism check fails, skip T0 and send it back to the engineer. Per-tier thresholds are unchanged (>= 39/48), and the logging is the same as X-025. Deviation: the pre-registration specifies a joint run on one commit, so the stages must stay on the same commit. on 12 scenes, 96 episodes. Expected learning: T1 is where the effect is biggest (0/48 baseline, 41/48 release failures), and it alone decides whether the lift clears the jaw. If T1 and its mechanism both fail, this saves 48 episodes. But T0 still carries independent information even then: whether the lift fixes the ground drag on T0-dev-05/03, a different contact geometry. And if the lift works, the stage-2 T0 run is required anyway. The prior from RS-005 (the defect is retreat geometry, and the code default of a pure vertical lift was never tested) favours the lift working, so the expected saving is small: roughly P(T1 and mechanism both fail) x 48.. Cost: 48-96 episodes, 96 expected. It adds one extra launch/analysis cycle. Each stage takes ~1 min of wall time, so there is essentially no wall-time saving, only a conditional episode saving.. 
- **X-028** [rejected] tests H-008 with v45_place_vlm (controls reused: v5_vlm R-016, v0 R-005, same scenes, seeds 0-1 subset) on 6 scenes, 12 episodes. Expected learning: Cheap mechanism probe. Only v45 runs, on T1-dev-01..06 with seeds 0-1. Controls come from existing runs with no new spend. Reads the per-scene b1 error against the H-008 predictions (dev-02 <=2.7 cm, dev-05 <=1.8 cm, dev-01 marginal at 2.8-3.3 cm). If dev-02 and dev-05 pass, the bias-removal mechanism is supported. If either fails, H-008 is refuted and continuous lateral perception comes next. Limit: n=6 scenes, and nothing about generalization to other T1 layouts or to off-grid T2/T3.. Cost: 12 episodes, about 1 min wall time on 8 workers (VLM outputs cached, weld build). Budget left after: 248.. 
- **X-029** [selected] tests H-008 with v45_place_vlm vs v5_vlm vs v0 (seeds 0-1, matched) on 20 scenes, 84 episodes. Expected learning: Scene-breadth test of H-008. Seeds 0-1 throughout. v45 runs on all 20 scenes (40 episodes). New v5_vlm controls run on T1-dev-07..12 and the off-grid scenes T2-dev-11..14 and T3-dev-13..16 (28 episodes). New v0 controls run on the off-grid scenes (16 episodes). Existing runs are reused for the other cells: v5_vlm R-016, v0 R-005 on T1-01..06, and v0 R-017 on T1-07..12. This takes T1 from n=6 to n=12 scenes. Off-grid T2/T3 is where VLM half-grid quantization should hurt most, so it is the real test of the 'perception budget = 3 cm - drift' claim. It also covers the H-008 no-regression requirement on T2/T3. If v45 reaches 12/12 T1 plus no off-grid regression, rung 5 is closed and v45 is promoted. If off-grid failures remain with b1 error close to |e|, the decision goes to continuous lateral perception instead of more placement work. Seeds 0-1 confirm seed invariance and cost only 2x per scene.. Cost: 84 episodes, about 3-5 min wall time on 8 workers (T2/T3 multi-block runs are slower). Budget left after: 176, which keeps the 48 reserved for X-019/X-022 plus 128 slack.. Outcomes look seed-invariant (R-016 = 4/6 scenes x 8 seeds, and R-017 and R-018 are identical), so the effective n is scenes. X-029 doubles T1 to 12 scenes and adds 8 off-grid T2/T3 scenes, where VLM quantization error peaks. That covers H-008's no-regression clause and separates 'promote v45' from 'move on to continuous lateral perception'. Every cell has a matched v0/v5_vlm control (new or reused R-005, R-016, R-017). Cost is 84 episodes over 3-5 min, leaving 176 with the 48 reserved for X-019/X-022 intact. X-028 (12 episodes) answers only the mechanism question on n=6 and would force a second run for generalization. X-030 (48 episodes) spends 36 episodes on seeds already known to be invariant and adds no new scenes.
- **X-030** [rejected] tests H-008 with v45_place_vlm (controls reused: v5_vlm R-016, v0 R-005, seeds 0-7) on 6 scenes, 48 episodes. Expected learning: The literal test filed in H-008: T1-dev-01..06 at seeds 0-7, fully matched to R-016 and R-005. It gives the same scene-level verdict as the cheap probe, because R-016's 32/48 split cleanly into 4/6 scenes x 8 seeds, which is evidence of seed invariance. The extra 36 episodes buy a tighter episode-level CI that we already know is pseudo-replicated. No new scenes and no generalization signal.. Cost: 48 episodes, about 1 min wall time. Budget left after: 212.. 
- **X-031** [rejected] tests H-009 with v2_walk with the heading fix (w_heading 8 -> 160 in lab/sumo_tasks/spot_walk_approach.py; same yaml name, so the commit must be pinned) on T0-dev-01..06, seeds 0-3, default physics: 24 eps. This is an exact rerun of the X-019 design. Control: v0 R-003, seed 0-3 rows, paired by (scene, seed), no new control spend (RS-004 bit-reproducible). Also compare with X-019 v2_walk on the same pairs to check that the 4 unreachable failures are fixed and that the heading weight has not degraded the 20 pairs that already passed. Log per episode: approach xy error (cm), yaw error (deg), final block pos/ang error, paired delta vs R-003, failure category. on 6 scenes, 24 episodes. Expected learning: This is the clean rung-2 verdict on T0 under the H-009 rule (NOT CLEARED, then AT RISK, then CLEARED) on exactly the pairs where X-019 failed. If 24/24 pass with yaw < 10 deg, a median paired delta <= 0.3 cm and max <= 2.7 cm, rung 2 is cleared on T0 and T1 comes next. Any unreachable failure means the heading term alone is not enough (look at CEM rollouts and episode length instead). AT RISK means re-targeting needs work before T1. Limit: nothing about stacked T1 tops, which are the real 3 cm stress test, so a second run is needed to climb tiers.. Cost: 24 eps (16% of the 151 left). About 82 s per episode end to end with Sumo, so about 3 waves on 8 workers, roughly 5-15 min. Don't run it alongside other batches (Sumo CPU contention costs wall time only).. 
- **X-032** [selected] tests H-009 with v2_walk with the heading fix (w_heading 160, commit pinned). Two arms:
Arm A: T0-dev-01..06, seeds 0-3, 24 eps (identical to X-031, judged under the H-009 rule) vs v0 R-003 seed 0-3 rows.
Arm B: T1-dev-01..06, seeds 0-1, 12 eps, vs v0 R-005 seed 0-1 rows (exploratory tier-climb probe unless the new hypothesis pre-registers T1 criteria).
Both are paired by (scene, seed), with no new control spend. Same per-episode logging as X-031, plus the top-block error on T1. Arm A gets its verdict on its own, so T1 results cannot rescue or sink the T0 verdict. on 12 scenes, 36 episodes. Expected learning: Arm A gives everything X-031 gives: a T0 rung-2 verdict on the exact X-019 failure pairs. Arm B answers the next question X-019's decision rule would ask anyway: does the walking base's approach error eat into the 3 cm budget on stacked tops? v0 is already at about 2.1 cm on T1. If the T1 paired delta is <= 0.3 cm with 12/12 passing, v2 can go straight into the combined-rung run (v1 support_sort + v2). If the delta is 0.3-0.8 cm or there are T1 failures with T0 clean, fix re-targeting from the true base pose before combining. If T0 fails, the 12 T1 episodes are mostly wasted, but they still show whether a failure is heading-specific or general. That is a small expected loss, since 3/3 smoke passes and the failure mechanism (yaw 77-179 deg) is directly targeted.. Cost: 36 eps (24% of 151 left; leaves about 115). About 5 waves on 8 workers at about 82 s per episode, roughly 8-20 min. Within the 40-episode cap.. X-032 (36 eps; 151 left, so about 115 remain) includes all of X-031: v2_walk with the heading fix on T0-dev-01..06, seeds 0-3, re-tests the exact pairs where X-019 failed against R-003. That arm gets its own verdict under H-009's rule. For 12 more episodes it adds a T1 seed 0-1 arm against R-005 rows. That arm answers the stacked-top 3 cm budget question, which the X-019 rule raises next anyway, and it saves a launch/analysis cycle on Sumo. The 3 smoke passes and the directly targeted 77-179 deg yaw mechanism make a wasted T1 arm unlikely.

Rejected X-031 (24 eps): same T0 verdict, but it forces a second Sumo run for T1.
Rejected X-033 (v1+v2 combined): no combined yaml exists so validate_method can't pass, and support_sort is a no-op on single-block T0, so it adds nothing over X-031 there.

Preconditions:
- The method_designer hypothesis must be filed before the first run (compare timestamps). It should state T1 criteria, otherwise arm B is exploratory.
- Pin the commit with w_heading=160, because the yaml is unchanged.
- No held-out scenes.
- **X-033** [rejected] tests H-009 with Combined rungs 1+2: a new config v12_sort_walk (plan_order=support_sort + build=sumo_walk_then_scripted_weld with the heading fix) on T0-dev-01..06, seeds 0-3, 24 eps. Controls: v0 R-003 (seed 0-3 rows), and the v2_walk heading-fix arm would be needed to attribute any change. on 6 scenes, 24 episodes. Expected learning: On T0, support_sort is a no-op: single blocks have no support order. So this run gives the same information as X-031 and adds nothing about combining rungs. Any failure would also confound v2's heading fix with the new config. The combination only becomes informative on T1-T3, and it should come after v2 is cleared on its own.. - **X-034** [rejected] tests H-012 with v2_walk (w_heading=160, frozen per H-012); control v0 reused from R-003 restricted to seeds 0-3 on 8 scenes, 28 episodes. Expected learning: (A) LITERAL H-012 RERUN. v2_walk on T0-dev-01..06 x seeds 0-3 (24 eps), plus T1-dev-01..02 x seeds 0-1 (4 eps) to finish the clean T1 arm (R-046 covered T1-dev-03..06). All runs max_workers=1, sumo_deterministic. It gives the pre-registered verdict exactly as worded: >=22/24 and every yaw <10 deg. Weakness: the walk ignores the scene, so the 24 T0 eps hold only 4 distinct walks (seeds 0-3), and the arm phase repeats 6 times per walk. Seeds 1 and 2 are the w_heading tuning seeds (H-012 caveat: T0-dev-02 s1/s2). Because the walk ignores the scene, seed-1 and seed-2 walks are in-sample in EVERY scene, so only 2 walks (seeds 0 and 3) are out of sample. Effectively the result is pass/fail on 2 new walks. A failure on seed 0 or 3 would refute H-012 (the walk fails 6/6 for that seed, giving <=18/24). A pass barely narrows the heading-failure rate: with 0/2 failures the 95% upper bound is about 78%. Decision impact: low. A pass would let the PI tick H-012 but would not support climbing to rung 3 with any confidence about the walk.. Cost: 28 budget episodes (113 left, so 85 remain). Wall time about 28 x 9 s = ~4.5 min at 1 worker (R-046: 72 s for 8 eps). Control is free (reused R-003, but on old commit cbd9b16, which is not matched to the current setup).. 
- **X-035** [selected] tests H-012 with v2_walk (w_heading=160, frozen) vs fresh matched v0 control on 8 scenes, 40 episodes. Expected learning: (B) DISTINCT-SEED WALK DISTRIBUTION, run as an H-012 amendment. method_designer must file the amendment BEFORE any run. Treatment: v2_walk on 16 distinct seeds 0-15, one episode per seed, round-robin over T0 (seed s -> T0-dev-0{(s mod 6)+1}), 16 eps. Plus T1-dev-01..02 x seeds 0-1 (4 eps) to finish the clean T1 arm. Control: fresh v0 on the same 16 T0 (scene, seed) pairs plus the same 4 T1 pairs (20 eps), same commit, max_workers=1. Suggested amended criterion (method_designer to finalise): >=15/16 T0 success, every walked yaw <10 deg, and out-of-sample walks (excluding seeds 1 and 2, the tuning seeds; in-sample in every scene because the walk ignores the scene) >=13/14. SECONDARY: walked XY <=2 cm, never >3 cm, and median paired block-position error change vs v0 <=+0.3 cm. What it gives: 16 distinct walks, 14 of them out of sample (vs 2 in A). That yields a real heading-error distribution (max, spread, how close to the 10 deg line) instead of a pass/fail. 0/14 failures puts the 95% upper bound on heading-failure rate at about 21%, vs about 78% for A. Decision impact: a clean pass supports moving to rung 3 with the walk treated as solved at T0. Any yaw >=10 deg, or yaw clustered near 10 deg, sends the walk back to method_designer before more budget goes into higher tiers. Each seed still runs the arm/block phase in a real scene, and all 6 T0 scenes get 2-3 episodes each.. Cost: 40 budget episodes, exactly the cap (113 left, so 73 remain). Wall time: v2_walk 20 x ~9 s = ~3 min at 1 worker; v0 20 eps take seconds. Total under 5 min.. Pick X-035 (B). It gives 14 out-of-sample walks for 40 episodes, and its v0 control matches on commit, scene and seed. A literal rerun (X-034) gives only 2 out-of-sample walks, because tuning seeds 1 and 2 are in-sample in every scene. Its 95% upper bound on heading-failure rate is about 78%, vs about 21% for B, and it relies on an unmatched historical control. X-036 matches B's walk count but spends 20 episodes on arm-phase repeats and has no fresh control (seeds 8-15 have no control pair at all). CONDITIONS before the first run: (1) method_designer files the H-012 amendment with a timestamp: seeds 0-15 one per scene round-robin, >=15/16 T0, every yaw <10 deg, out-of-sample >=13/14, w_heading freeze unchanged; (2) runner confirms explicit (scene, seed) pairs, or splits into per-scene runs; (3) max_workers=1, dev scenes only. Follow-up for scene_designer: the walk start/goal is fixed and never varies with the scene. This is a test-coverage gap, out of scope here. Budget: 113 -> 73 after this run.

- **X-037** [selected] tests H-013 with v46_vlm_measure vs v0. PART A (40 eps, all fresh v46): T1-dev-01..12 (confirmatory) + old off-grid T2-dev-11..14, T3-dev-13..16 (exploratory), seeds 0-1. v0 controls reused and paired by (scene, seed): R-005 (T1-01..06), R-017 (T1-07..12), R-035 (off-grid 8). PART B (32 eps): fresh confirmatory off-grid T2-dev-15..18, T3-dev-17..20, seeds 0-1, v46 (16) + fresh v0 (16), same commit. No v46 run on Part B scenes before this experiment (query_record kind=run contains=v46 was empty at filing). on 28 scenes, 72 episodes. Expected learning: BASELINE (A+B). The confirmatory rung-5 verdict vs v0 only (amendment 1) comes from T1 24 eps plus the 16 fresh off-grid eps in Part B. These scenes were never used to tune the white-mask fix, which the reviewer flagged on an off-grid gate scene. Part A off-grid (16 eps) keeps the pre-registered mechanism clauses checkable: the staircase b2 on T2-dev-11 / T3-dev-14 with e > 3 cm is a REFUTED clause, and so is the off-grid median |e|. Those results are exploratory because of the Gate 0 contamination. They also tie back to RS-012's failure set. 'BEATS v45' (R-033) is exploratory only. Decisions: off-grid fresh >= 14/16 with T1 >= 23/24 and median |e| <= 1.0 cm supports clearing rung 5 on off-grid. Median |e| > 1.0 cm with a success gain attributes the gain to placement, not perception. Old-vs-fresh off-grid divergence (old passes, fresh fails) flags overfitting of the mask fix. Per-block split logged: e = perceived y - true y, and placement = final - perceived target.. Cost: 72 episodes (56 v46 + 16 v0). Budget 113 - 32 reserved X-035 = 81 usable, so 9 slack remains after this run. That covers the optional +8 seed-2 add-on but nothing else. Wall time: about 72 x 0.5-3 s of sim on 8 workers (~1-4 min), plus 56 fresh VLM calls (cache_mode fresh_per_seed) at 10-60 s each (~10-55 min, dominant). No Sumo.. X-037 (72 eps; 113 left, 32 reserved for X-035, so 81 usable and 9 slack after) is the only design that gives a clean confirmatory verdict vs v0 on scenes the mask fix never saw (T1 24 + fresh off-grid 16 with a fresh matched v0). It also keeps the pre-registered staircase-b2 and median-|e| REFUTED clauses checkable on the old off-grid scenes, reported as exploratory because of the Gate 0 contamination. X-038 saves 16 eps but drops a pre-registered refutation clause after Gate 0 was seen, and it loses the old-vs-fresh overfitting check. X-039 saves 8 eps by imputing v0 seed 1 on the confirmatory arm, which is the arm that most needs a measured control. PRECONDITIONS before the first episode: (1) amend H-013 with a timestamp: 72-ep cap (the record says 60), Part B scenes confirmatory, Gate 0 and old off-grid exploratory, verdict vs v0 only, 'beats v45' exploratory; (2) clean committed tree, checked by the runner preflight, with the commit hash reported; (3) v46 perceive fresh per (scene, seed), with the cached flag + hash logged; (4) a pre-run scene-spec check that the fresh scenes are off the 6.5 cm grid and >=2 are staircases. Labels alone (tower/bridge/L/T/pyramid) can't confirm either. If <2 are staircases, the confirmatory off-grid arm cannot test the staircase mechanism and must say so. Dev only. The optional +8 seed-2 add-on fits the 9 slack exactly; nothing else does.

Arm B (CONFIRMATORY, 16 pairs x2 = 32): 8 brand-new off-grid scenes from scene_designer (likely T2-dev-19..22, T3-dev-21..24; ids TBD, not yet in scene list so not in scene_ids; must exist and pass scene-spec check before commit freeze), v46 vs fresh v0, seeds 0-1. H-013 verdict rests on Arm B vs matched v0 only. Never held-out scenes. on 28 scenes, 144 episodes. Expected learning: Arm B is the first uncontaminated off-grid test of the white-mask fix; Arm A shows gate regressions and exploratory off-grid effect. Fresh v0 on every pair removes historical-control drift.. Cost: 144 episodes (112 + 32). - **X-046** [selected] tests H-016 with v2_walk_xy + v2_walk_settle (stage 1 screen; stage 2 conditional: winner vs v2_walk vs v0) on 6 scenes, 32 episodes. Expected learning: H-016 pre-registered design. STAGE 1 (32 eps, committed now): one run_sim_batch per method (v2_walk_xy, v2_walk_settle), nominal start, T0 seeds 0-15, scene T0-dev-((s mod 6)+1), paired by (scene, seed) with R-080 (v2_walk) as reference. Eligibility: all 16 walks XY<=3 cm AND yaw<10 deg; lower max XY wins; tie (<0.1 cm) -> lower median XY; then A. Neither eligible -> H-016 REFUTED, stage 2 not run.
DISCLOSURE (must precede any screen run): smoke at varied seed 16 (single episode, no pre-registered verdict): A ended 1.85 deg yaw, final XY ~0.7 cm lateral; B ended 37.3 deg yaw because its large w_xy_quad=2e4 term overrides w_heading=160. H-016 predicted B would be picked (max yaw<7 deg); the smoke already contradicts the yaw prediction for B. Consequences: (1) B is likely INELIGIBLE under the yaw<10 rule, so the screen probably reduces to A passing/failing; it is still run in full because the rule is pre-registered and one episode at a varied start is not nominal-start evidence; (2) seed 16 was seen in smoke, so it is no longer blind in stage 2 (only the smoke's seed/arm, not a stage-2 verdict; stage 2 remains out-of-sample for 23 of 24 poses and seed 16 is flagged); (3) the smoke is NOT a selection input and the selection rule is unchanged. No gains/terms change (freeze).
Decision value: picks A or B on the same nominal poses as R-080 and tests whether B's yaw failure is nominal or only varied-start. Gate for 84 stage-2 episodes.
Stage 2 (conditional, filed as the next experiment after stage 1 result): winner on seeds 16-39 (T0-dev-((s mod 6)+1)) + T1-dev-01/02 x seeds {16,23} = 28 pairs; control v2_walk (unchanged) and fresh v0, same 28 pairs, same commit = 84 eps, one run_sim_batch per method. Total 116.. Cost: Stage 1: 32 eps, ~8-13 s each at max_workers=1 (pre-registered) = ~5-7 min wall. Full design 116 eps ~20-25 min. Budget 1005 left, not binding.. Follow H-016's pre-registered two-stage design. The nominal screen of A vs B (32 eps) is the only stage whose selection rule and decision rule are already frozen. Stage 2 (84 eps: winner, v2_walk, v0 on the same 28 pairs) is then run on the winner only. Straight-to-varied (X-047, 112 eps) would need an unfiled amendment, spends 28 eps on B, whose smoke yaw of 37.3 deg already looks ineligible, and uses up the varied seeds. Budget is not a factor (1005 eps left), and no design was shrunk: the full 116 eps are retained. The B smoke-yaw and seed-16 disclosures are in X-046 and precede any screen run.

It cannot show whether A/B fix the original nominal s6/s4 tail the hypothesis was filed for. Would change the decision only if both candidates fail varied starts, which stage 1 + 2 would also reveal at lower risk.. Cost: 112 eps, ~8-13 s each at max_workers=1 = ~15-25 min wall. Not budget-limited (1005 left)..

A single run gives the cleanest attribution for the whole ladder.. Cost: 288 episodes (501 -> 213, a 42% margin). Sumo wall time is about 1.5-2.6 h in total, serial. The v0 arm takes minutes.. X-061 runs v0, v2_walk and v6_combo on the same 96 T2/T3 pairs at frozen 76cc1a7. It gives each of (a) and (b) a full 48-per-tier test against one shared fresh v0. That costs 288 episodes, against 384 for running (a) and (b) separately (X-059 + X-060), which leaves 213 of 501 as margin. Identical pairs also let a combo failure be compared with the walk-only result on the same pair. X-062 is a shrunken screen that can't clear anything, so it is rejected. Sumo arms run serially at max_workers=1. Both pre-registrations must be filed before the freeze, and the v6_combo arm can be dropped if the H-020 review fails.

It would also use seeds and scenes that a later full test needs to keep fresh, and the full test would then have to run anyway.. Cost: 144 episodes, with no verdict at the end. This is a net loss against (c)..

## Hypotheses (agent-generated, labeled by author)

- **H-001** [supported] by method_designer: Replacing the oracle build order with symbolic_order_planner (plan_order=support_sort: support-graph topological sort + settle test per prefix), all other stages as in v0, keeps dev success at the v0 level because with an exact oracle spec, a support-respecting order is enough for the scripted weld build. (predicted: Dev success >= 0.9 overall on T0-T3 dev scenes (24 scenes), and >= 0.8 on every tier (T2 bridge and T3 pyramid included), within 0.05 of v0 on each tier. Falsified if any tier's dev success < 0.8, or if the planned order differs from a valid support order (block placed before its supporter) on any scene.; evidence: E-001, E-002, E-003, E-004)
- **H-002** [refuted] by method_designer: Replacing the oracle structure spec with VLM parsing (perceive=vlm, call_model image->JSON with block type + pose), with oracle order and build kept, fails the 3 cm / 10 deg criterion on multi-block scenes, because VLMs detect blocks well but are weak at fine-grained 3D pose and orientation. (predicted: Dev success >= 0.8 on T0, but <= 0.5 on T2 and <= 0.3 on T3 dev scenes; block count/type correct on >= 0.9 of scenes while pose error > 3 cm or > 10 deg on >= 1 block in most failures. Falsified (in the optimistic direction) if T2 and T3 dev success both reach >= 0.8 with VLM-only parsing; falsified (pessimistic) if T0 < 0.8.; evidence: E-001, E-005, E-006, E-007)
- **H-003** [open] by method_designer: Hybrid rung-5 perception (VLM for block count, type, color and support relations, followed by a geometric contact snap: x=0, z=0.05+0.10*layer, bottom layer on the 0.13 m contact grid centered at y=0, bridge blocks at the supporters' midpoint, single-support blocks keep the VLM's lateral offset) matches but does not beat VLM-only parsing. Reason: dev eval_prompt on prompts/perceive/v1 with codex (24 dev images, 2026-10-04) already gives count_accuracy 1.0, all blocks within 3 cm, and max_pos_err 0.0-0.6 cm on T0/T2/T3. The only residual error is the continuous lateral offset of stacked blocks on T1 (max 0.5-2.8 cm), which grid snapping cannot recover: snapping it to 0 would raise T1 error up to 3 cm (stack) or 6 cm (cube_on_brick). E-006/E-007 predicted the main risk was fine pose and orientation. At this benchmark's scale the risk is concentrated in sub-block lateral offsets, and yaw is 0 by construction. REFUTED for (a) (the drift is controller error, not creep) if the median error changes by < 0.3 cm and success is identical scene for scene. (b) We predict fewer v3_grasp slip/drop failures after re-tuning under elliptic + impratio 10. In the v3 arms, the re-tune and the physics change happen together, so the two are confounded and we can't credit the drop to creep alone. At most, (b) can be WEAKLY SUPPORTED by a drop in slip/drop count. - **H-006** [reopened] by method_designer: [X-014 | rung 4 | filed before any X-014 / v4_place results were read] v4_place (methods/v4_place.yaml, build=closed_loop_weld_place, lab/stages/closed_loop_place.py) removes the scripted arm's systematic placement offset. RS-001/RS-002 found that offset to be a ~1.5-1.9 cm lateral bias whose sign follows the pick side (b_y = +1.5 / -1.9 / -1.6 cm on T1-dev-01/02/05), and it is behind every v0 T2/T3 placement_error. Mechanism: at the place waypoint the stage reads the welded block's pose, computes residual = (SITE + spec.pos + clearance) - actual, and commands up to 2 IK tip corrections (max step 4 cm, tolerance 1 mm) before release. Because the block is welded and arm-block collision is off, the measured residual should move the block one-to-one, leaving only release/settle drift. IDEALIZATION: the correction reads the TRUE block pose from the sim (oracle_block_pose; no camera, depth or state estimator). A rung-4 pass therefore shows that the bias is correctable, not that it can be sensed. The other idealizations kept from v0: oracle_structure_spec, oracle_build_order, fixed_base, weld_grasp, no_arm_block_collision. Alternatives considered: sampling-based MPC (v4_mpc, Sumo CEM) was rejected for this question. It costs about 600 s per episode on CPU against a 24 h budget, and a deterministic 2 cm translation offset needs feedback, not search. A calibrated per-side feed-forward offset was rejected because it overfits the pick side seen in dev and would not transfer to held-out start poses. (predicted: X-014: v4_place, seeds 0-3, on the 34 T1-T3 dev scenes (136 episodes), compared against matched v0 on the same scenes and seeds.

(3) No tier regresses against v0. Per tier, v4 successes >= v0 successes on the same scene x seed pairs, and no scene flips from pass in v0 to fail in v4.

REFUTED if any of these occur: the median error is >= 1.5 cm on any tier; a scene passes in v0 but fails in v4 (the correction or release disturbs the structure); any tier's LB < 0.8; or pre_release_err is small (<= 0.3 cm) while the final error stays >= 1.5 cm. The last case would mean the bias comes from release/settle (creep, see H-005), not from arm positioning, and the next step is a release fix rather than more correction.

- **H-013** [open] by method_designer: [Rung 5 | agent hypothesis by method_designer | SUCCESSOR to H-008 (refuted by RS-012), and SUPERSEDES H-003 | filed BEFORE any run of v46_vlm_measure. A query_record(kind=run, contains="measure") just before filing was empty, and the newest run was R-046 (v2_walk). Confirm by comparing this record's created timestamp with the earliest v46_vlm_measure run.]
CLAIM: v46_vlm_measure removes the lateral quantization error e that dominates the off-grid failures, and so closes most of the off-grid T2/T3 gap to v0 without losing T1. It splits perception in two. The VLM gives STRUCTURE only: block id, color, type, layer, and supported_by ids. A geometric step MEASURES each block's continuous lateral position y from the target picture.
MECHANISM: per RS-012, 5/9 v45 off-grid failures are perception-dominated. They are staircases with 1.8-2.2 cm offsets per level, collapsed to vertical stacks (e = 4.0-4.5 cm), plus one wrong-cell snap (e = 4.4 cm). Another 3/9 are marginal: e = 2.7-2.85 cm, combined in quadrature with the x shortfall of about 1.5 cm. Prompt v1 itself invites the snapping: it says 'cube centers 0.13 m apart' and asks for absolute positions, and the VLM rounds to half a grid step. Every block has a unique, saturated color on a clean render, and structures are one block deep, so the side elevation view in scene.image_views (image axes = structure y, z) shows each block as an unoccluded colored rectangle. The block's mask centroid u, scaled by pixels-per-metre, gives y. The scale comes from the known block widths (cube 0.12 m, brick 0.24 m) measured in the same image, so no camera intrinsics are needed. Expected |e| <= 1 cm, giving a total of sqrt(1^2 + 1.5^2) = 1.8 cm, under the 3 cm tolerance.
SINGLE CHANGE vs v45 (R-033): only the perceive stage changes. plan_order=oracle, build=closed_loop_weld_place and all build params are unchanged, so a difference vs R-033 is attributable to perception.
ALTERNATIVES REJECTED:
(i) H-003's geometric grid snap makes quantization worse: it snaps the 2 cm offsets to 0, the exact failure seen.
(ii) A prompt-only 'continuous cm offsets' v2 cannot be ruled out, but VLMs are weak metric estimators, and the outputs vary per seed (perceive.cached=false; T2-dev-14 s0 vs s1). It is tested FREE as a perception-only arm (eval_prompt), not in sim, unless it matches the geometric measurement.
(iii) Rendered depth or sim state as input violates 'given only a picture'.
(iv) MPC, training and VLA are not relevant to a perception error, or are not installed.
IDEALIZATIONS STILL IN USE: oracle_build_order, oracle_block_pose (the closed-loop correction reads sim truth), fixed_base, weld_grasp, scripted_arm, no_arm_block_collision. Also, newly explicit: clean_render_color_segmentation (distinct saturated colors, fixed lighting, near-orthographic side view), plus x=0, yaw=0, z snapped to layer.
KNOWN CONFOUND: H-006 is reopened, and the 1.5 cm x shortfall may be nondeterministic or depend on the commit. Run on a clean, committed tree and report the placement residual (final minus perceived target) per block, separately from e. (predicted: GATE 0 (free, before any episode; eval / offline script on dev ground truth): on the 20 scenes (T1-dev-01..12, T2-dev-11..14, T3-dev-13..16), the measured lateral error is max |e| <= 1.5 cm and median <= 0.7 cm over all blocks. The VLM structure (count, type, color, layer, supporter) is correct on >= 19/20 scenes on each of 2 sampled calls. If Gate 0 fails, do NOT spend episodes: revise and file a successor.
PRIMARY SIM TEST (X-new): v46_vlm_measure on the same 20 scenes, seeds 0-1 = 40 episodes. Paired by (scene, seed) with reused controls: v45 = R-033 (seeds 0-1, all 20 scenes); v0 = R-035 (off-grid seeds 0-1) and R-005/R-017 (T1 seed 0-1 rows). Unit of analysis = episode (scene x seed), because the VLM is resampled per seed (RS-012 surprise 3). Also report scene level (a scene passes only if both seeds pass) and Wilson 95% CIs.
PREDICTION: off-grid >= 14/16 episodes (v45 7/16, v0 15/16). T1 24/24. Off-grid median per-block |e| <= 0.7 cm, and no block with e > 2 cm.
DECISION RULE, checked in order:
1. VOID: the stage reads scene.spec or sim state for y, or the tree is not clean.
2. REFUTED if ANY of: off-grid <= 10/16; T1 <= 22/24; a T1 scene that passed both seeds in R-033 fails both seeds; off-grid median |e| > 1.5 cm (the mechanism fails, whatever the success rate); or the staircase b2 on T2-dev-11 or T3-dev-14 still has e > 3 cm in either seed.
3. PARTIAL if off-grid is 11-13/16, T1 >= 23/24, and median |e| <= 1.0 cm. Perception is fixed, but residual failures are structural mis-parses or the x shortfall. The successor targets whichever dominates, and is not more lateral perception.
4. SUPPORTED if off-grid >= 14/16, T1 >= 23/24, and every failing block has |e| <= 1.5 cm.
5. 'BEATS v45' is claimed only if, among the 16 off-grid pairs, >= 6 discordant favor v46 and 0 favor v45 (exact McNemar p = 0.031). 'MATCHES v0' means off-grid >= v0 - 1 (>= 14/16), and is reported as statistically unresolved at n = 16.
COST: 40 episodes primary. Optional add-on, run only if PARTIAL or SUPPORTED: seed 2 on the 8 off-grid scenes, treatment only (+8), reported descriptively. Total <= 48 episodes (cap 60), plus about 40 VLM calls (10-60 s each) and free eval_prompt calls for Gate 0.; evidence: RS-012, R-033, R-035, R-005, R-017, H-008, H-003, RS-010, H-006)

WHAT IS AMENDED: only the design, thresholds and decision rule of H-012. The claim and mechanism are unchanged: w_heading 8 -> 160 closes the 77-179 deg yaw failures of X-019 without hurting XY. The FREEZE is unchanged: w_heading = 160, and no other walk gain changes after H-012 was filed. Any change voids the test. This supersedes H-012's 24-episode literal design (X-034, rejected) for the purpose of judging H-012.
METHOD: methods/v2_walk.yaml unchanged (perceive=oracle, plan_order=oracle, build=sumo_walk_then_scripted_weld, Sumo spot_walk_approach, Relic, CEM 24 rollouts). w_heading=160 lives in lab/sumo_tasks/spot_walk_approach.py and must be at a CLEAN COMMIT. Approaches rejected: MPC for heading costs ~600 s per episode and is not needed for a gain fix. Retuning is forbidden by the freeze. Training is not allowed.
DESIGN (32 episodes):
TREATMENT, v2_walk, 20 eps:
- T0: seeds s = 0..15, one episode each, on T0-dev-((s mod 6)+1). Mapping: s0,6,12 -> dev-01; s1,7,13 -> dev-02; s2,8,14 -> dev-03; s3,9,15 -> dev-04; s4,10 -> dev-05; s5,11 -> dev-06. That is 16 eps.
- T1: T1-dev-01 and T1-dev-02, each with seeds 0 and 1. That is 4 eps.
CONTROL, v0, same (scene, seed) pairs:
- REUSED, per PI overrule: R-003 rows (T0-dev-01 s0, T0-dev-02 s1, T0-dev-03 s2, T0-dev-04 s3) and R-005 rows (T1-dev-01/02 x s0/s1). That is 8 pairs, all historical, with R-003 on old commit cbd9b16 and 8 workers. The reuse is justified only by v0 bit-reproducibility (RS-004, R-026, R-027).
- FRESH: v0 at the X-035 commit for the 12 T0 pairs, seeds 4-15. That is 12 eps.
EFFECTIVE n: the walk ignores the scene, so the walk sample is 16 distinct seeds. Seeds 1 and 2 are w_heading tuning seeds and count as in-sample everywhere. OUT-OF-SAMPLE set = T0 seeds {0, 3..15}, n = 14. The 4 T1 episodes repeat the walks of seeds 0 and 1. They are NOT added to the walk n. They serve as the determinism check and the T1 arm-phase check.
Primary variables, logged for every treatment episode: walked yaw error |deg|, walked XY error cm, success, failure category, and wall time / timeout. (predicted: FINAL THRESHOLDS AND DECISION RULE. Rules are checked in this order, and the first rule that fires decides: VOID, then NOT CLEARED, then AT RISK, then CLEARED. A "heading failure" is any treatment episode with walked yaw >= 10 deg.
1. VOID if ANY of these hold:
- The tree is dirty, or the treatment and fresh-v0 commits differ.
- w_heading != 160, or any walk gain differs from H-012's frozen values.
- max_workers != 1, or deterministic Sumo is not enabled.
- Any (scene, seed) pair deviates from the mapping above.
- Walked yaw or XY is missing for any treatment episode.
- DETERMINISM CHECK FAILS: the walk ignores the scene, so the walked final base pose for seed 0 must match between T0-dev-01 s0, T1-dev-01 s0 and T1-dev-02 s0, and for seed 1 between T0-dev-02 s1, T1-dev-01 s1 and T1-dev-02 s1. Tolerance is 1e-6 m and 1e-4 deg. A mismatch means the walk is not deterministic, so seeds cannot be treated as the unit of analysis.
2. NOT CLEARED (this REFUTES amended H-012) if ANY of these hold:
- (a) T0 success <= 14/16.
- (b) Any treatment episode has walked yaw >= 10 deg. This covers all 20 episodes, successes and failures alike.
- (c) Out-of-sample T0 (seeds 0 and 3-15) <= 12/14.
- (d) Any 'unreachable' failure on T0 or T1.
- (e) Walked XY > 3 cm in any episode. This refutes the 'no side effect' mechanism even if success holds.
If (a) fires and every failure has yaw < 10 deg, the heading fix worked but some other defect limits success. That is recorded under a successor, not counted as support.
3. AT RISK (no NOT CLEARED condition holds, but ANY of these do):
- A T0 failure, i.e. exactly 15/16.
- Any T1 failure.
- Max walked yaw >= 7 deg, which is clustered near the 10 deg line. Post-fix smoke yaws were 2.1-2.4 deg.
- Any walked XY in (2, 3] cm.
- Median paired block-position error change vs v0 > +0.3 cm, over all 20 pairs.
- Any 600 s timeout.
4. CLEARED: 16/16 T0, 4/4 T1, every walked yaw < 7 deg, every walked XY <= 2 cm, and median paired block-error change <= +0.3 cm.
PREDICTION (method_designer's, not a decision): CLEARED. Expected yaws are 1-4 deg, with max < 5 deg.
REPORTING, all required:
(i) Walked-yaw distribution over the 16 distinct T0 walks: the sorted list with seed labels and in/out-of-sample tags, median, IQR, max, counts >= 5 / >= 7 / >= 10 deg, and the min margin to 10 deg. Repeat the list for the 14 out-of-sample walks. T1 yaws are listed separately as determinism duplicates.
(ii) Exact Clopper-Pearson two-sided 95% upper bound on the heading-failure rate p(yaw >= 10 deg), with the walk seed as the unit. At 0/16 the bound is 20.6% (one-sided 95%: 17.1%). At 0/14 out-of-sample it is 23.2% (one-sided: 19.3%). Correction to X-035's text: its "~21% at 0/14" is the 16-walk two-sided figure. With k > 0 failures, report the exact bound for k.
(iii) Paired block-error change vs v0, split into reused pairs (R-003/R-005, n = 8) and fresh pairs (n = 12). If the medians of the two splits disagree in sign by more than 0.3 cm, flag that reuse may be invalid. This is diagnostic only.
(iv) Success with Wilson 95% CIs, while noting that effective n = 16 walks.
SCOPE: the walk start/goal is a fixed nominal pose, so a pass shows heading robustness to seed only, not to the start pose (scene_designer follow-up). No retuning of any kind is permitted. CONTEXT: D-010 voided X-037/RS-016 because the code fingerprint differed between its parts. Part A ran on 80d4b35 (ws 3f9b7a). Part B ran on ws e00db7, and R-078 (1c4abe0) and R-079 (aa5cc95) also differ in git_head. RS-016 (now void) also showed that per-block perceived y was not logged, and that T2-dev-16 (both seeds) failed through a deterministic 6-7 cm lateral mis-measurement of the white cube on a bridge, with T2-dev-18 also involving the white cube. The H-013 claim and mechanism are unchanged. Only the items below change.
(A1) MASK GENERALISATION (robotics_engineer, being implemented in lab/stages/vlm_measure.py). The white cube has low saturation, so the saturated-colour mask treated it as background or merged it with the bridge. The new mask segments white or near-white blocks by a low-saturation, high-value threshold against the known render background. When a white cube supports a bridge brick or sits on one, the bridge-brick mask edge splits it from the cube, so the cube centroid is measured from its own pixels and not from the merged blob. Pixel-per-metre scale still comes from the known block widths in the same image. No scene.spec or sim state is read for y (this is still VOID clause 1). It is a perception-only change: build/placement code is unchanged from the frozen commit.
(A2) LOGGED METRIC, pre-registered: every block in every v46 episode logs e = perceived y - true y (cm, signed), and the placement residual p = final y - perceived target y. Ground truth is used for scoring only, after perception. Report median |e| and max |e| per arm, plus per block on white-cube blocks.
(A3) FROZEN COMMIT: one clean, committed tree, with an identical run_fingerprint.id (same git_head AND workspace_sha256) across ALL arms run in the rerun (v46 Part A, v46 Part B, v0 Part B). Commit the mask fix first, then freeze. VOID if any rerun run's fingerprint differs from the first, if the tree is dirty, or if any v46 episode lacks the cached/fresh flag, the VLM reading hash, or per-block e.
(A4) DESIGN, identical to the 72-episode X-037 design: Part A, v46: T1-dev-01..12 seeds 0-1 (24 eps, CONFIRMATORY, absolute threshold, v0 reference R-005/R-017) plus old off-grid T2-dev-11..14 and T3-dev-13..16 seeds 0-1 (16 eps, EXPLORATORY). Part B: T2-dev-15..18 and T3-dev-17..20 seeds 0-1, v46 (16) vs a FRESH v0 (16) at the same frozen commit, paired by (scene, seed). Total 72. No add-on.
(A5) CONTAMINATION DISCLOSURE: the mask fix targets a failure first seen on Part B scenes T2-dev-16/18. Part B is therefore no longer fully unseen. The verdict still uses all 16 Part B pairs as pre-registered, but the report MUST also give the sensitivity readout on the 12 Part B episodes excluding T2-dev-16/18 (descriptive, not a verdict). (predicted: THRESHOLDS UNCHANGED from H-013 as amended for X-037. Part B off-grid v46: predicted >= 14/16. SUPPORTED >= 14/16 (and every failing block |e| <= 1.5 cm). PARTIAL 11-13/16. REFUTED <= 10/16. T1 (Part A): predicted 24/24, REFUTED <= 22/24, SUPPORTED/PARTIAL require >= 23/24. Verdict is against v0 only. v45 comparison is exploratory. McNemar 'beats v0' is reported, not required.
e-PREDICTIONS (pre-registered, now measurable): Part B median |e| <= 0.7 cm, max |e| <= 1.5 cm. White-cube blocks on T2-dev-16 and T2-dev-18: |e| <= 1.5 cm in both seeds (was 6-7 cm on T2-dev-16). Mechanism REFUTED (whatever the success rate) if Part B median |e| > 1.5 cm, or if any white-cube block has |e| > 3 cm. If success improves but Part B median |e| > 1.0 cm, the gain is attributed to placement, not perception. If failures persist with |e| <= 1.5 cm and |p| > 3 cm, they are attributed to placement (H-006), not perception.
Exploratory readouts: old off-grid 16 eps (staircase b2 on T2-dev-11/T3-dev-14, e < 3 cm), and Part B without T2-dev-16/18 (12 eps).
COST: 72 episodes of the 501 remaining (429 left after). About 56 VLM calls.; evidence: H-013, RS-016, R-078, R-079, R-005, R-017, R-035, R-033, H-006)
